# TCG Battle Attack Support Audit

Generated from all 217 catalogued sets using the set-aware attack resolver. The catalog contains 20,315 Pokémon cards and 31,296 attack occurrences. 27,875 attacks match an implemented effect; 3,421 occurrences across 2,756 set-specific text groups have no resolver mapping and need review. Matching a text family does not prove every printed rider is implemented; this report only audits whether an attack is offered by the current set-aware resolver.

Cards listed below have printed attacks but no attack currently supported by the resolver; the card may therefore have no usable attack in battle.
